What Changed

Added real triplanar base/silt sampling with hard blend weights in 
Hecton_AbyssalVoxelRock.shader (line 318)
:
half3 ResolveTriplanarBlendWeights(half3 normalWS)
{
    half3 blend = pow(max(abs(normalWS), half3(0.0001h, 0.0001h, 0.0001h)), max((half)_TriplanarBlendSharpness, 8.0h));
    return blend / max(dot(blend, half3(1.0h, 1.0h, 1.0h)), 0.0001h);
}

half4 SampleTriplanarColor(TEXTURE2D_PARAM(tex, samp), float3 positionWS, half3 normalWS)
{
    float tiling = max(_Tiling, 0.0001);
    half3 blend = ResolveTriplanarBlendWeights(normalWS);
    half4 xSample = SAMPLE_TEXTURE2D(tex, samp, positionWS.zy * tiling);
    half4 ySample = SAMPLE_TEXTURE2D(tex, samp, positionWS.xz * tiling);
    half4 zSample = SAMPLE_TEXTURE2D(tex, samp, positionWS.xy * tiling);
    return (xSample * blend.x) + (ySample * blend.y) + (zSample * blend.z);
}
Kept world-space GPU jagged displacement via existing ValueNoise3 path and cave-mouth displacement.
Added cheap cave-mouth AO multiply from distance-derived COLOR.a / skirt alpha at shader lines 820-822.
Silt dust now uses triplanar silt texture on upward normals at line 824.
Set laser debris lifetime to 5s in 
VoxelDeltaProcessor.cs (line 66)
.
Debris now uses authored sink distance/duration, disables simulated collision, and settles as instanced static state in 
DebrisManager.cs (line 432)
.
Removed all mesh.vertices / mesh.normals / mesh.triangles direct calls under Assets/_Project/Scripts.
Recon Results

No JobHandle.Complete() found in the scanned voxel cancellation/commit paths. VoxelDeltaProcessor uses non-forcing DispatcherJobSwap.TryComplete(..., false).
Late-frame carve commit already capped at 64 writes: MaxScheduledCarveCommitWritesPerFrame = 64.
VoxelDynamicNavGridRuntime.ResetRuntime() routes to DisposeAll(); pending jobs are deferred via non-forcing completion and SystemDispatcher polls completion.
VoxelNormalJob is central-difference based, but expensive: it interpolates 8 grid-node gradients. No profiler evidence was available showing >1.0 ms, so I did not activate a fallback.

Surgery Log

float ResolveOrganicVertexDisplacement(float3 absolutePositionWS, float3 normalOS, half seamMask)
{
    float scale = max(_OrganicDisplacementScale, 0.02);
    float lowNoise = ValueNoise3(absolutePositionWS * scale + 3.17);
    float midNoise = ValueNoise3(absolutePositionWS * (scale * 2.03) + 19.71);
    float fineNoise = ValueNoise3(absolutePositionWS * max(_OrganicDisplacementFineScale, 0.05) + 53.2);
    float ridged = abs(((lowNoise * 0.54 + midNoise * 0.31 + fineNoise * 0.15) * 2.0) - 1.0);
    float signedRidge = (ridged * 2.0) - 1.0;
    float slopeGate = saturate(1.0 - abs(normalOS.y) * 0.28);
    float seamBoost = 1.0 + saturate(seamMask) * _OrganicDisplacementSeamBoost;
    return signedRidge * _OrganicDisplacementStrength * seamBoost * slopeGate;
}
Verification

float ValueNoise3(float3 value) { ... }

float ResolveOrganicVertexDisplacement(float3 absolutePositionWS, float3 normalOS, half seamMask)
{
    float scale = max(_OrganicDisplacementScale, 0.02);
    float lowNoise = ValueNoise3(absolutePositionWS * scale + 3.17);
    float midNoise = ValueNoise3(absolutePositionWS * (scale * 2.03) + 19.71);
    float fineNoise = ValueNoise3(absolutePositionWS * max(_OrganicDisplacementFineScale, 0.05) + 53.2);
    float ridged = abs(((lowNoise * 0.54 + midNoise * 0.31 + fineNoise * 0.15) * 2.0) - 1.0);
    float signedRidge = (ridged * 2.0) - 1.0;
    float slopeGate = saturate(1.0 - abs(normalOS.y) * 0.28);
    float seamBoost = 1.0 + saturate(seamMask) * _OrganicDisplacementSeamBoost;
    return signedRidge * _OrganicDisplacementStrength * seamBoost * slopeGate;
}

FNV-1a kernel in 
QuestRuntimeTypes.cs (line 165)
:

internal static class QuestFlagHashKernel
{
    public static uint ComputeStableHash(ReadOnlySpan<char> value)
    {
        if (value.Length <= 0)
            return 0u;

        unchecked
        {
            uint hash = Hecton.Localization.LocHash.FnvOffsetBasis;
            for (int i = 0; i < value.Length; i++)
            {
                char current = value[i];
                hash ^= (byte)current;
                hash *= Hecton.Localization.LocHash.FnvPrime;
                hash ^= (byte)(current >> 8);
                hash *= Hecton.Localization.LocHash.FnvPrime;
            }

            return hash;
        }
    }
}

FNV-1a kernel in 
QuestRuntimeTypes.cs (line 165)
:

internal static class QuestFlagHashKernel
{
    public static uint ComputeStableHash(string value)
    {
        return string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(value)
            ? 0u
            : ComputeStableHash(value.AsSpan());
    }

    public static uint ComputeStableHash(ReadOnlySpan<char> value)
    {
        unchecked
        {
            uint hash = Hecton.Localization.LocHash.FnvOffsetBasis;
            for (int i = 0; i < value.Length; i++)
            {
                char current = value[i];
                hash ^= (byte)current;
                hash *= Hecton.Localization.LocHash.FnvPrime;
                hash ^= (byte)(current >> 8);
                hash *= Hecton.Localization.LocHash.FnvPrime;
            }
            return hash;
        }
    }
}

double distanceSq = AbsoluteUniversePosition.DistanceSq(in playerAup, in coreAup);

:

[BurstCompile]
internal static unsafe class QuestFlagHashKernel
{
    public static uint ComputeStableHash(ReadOnlySpan<char> value)
    {
        if (value.Length <= 0)
            return 0u;

        fixed (char* valuePtr = value)
            return ComputeStableHash(valuePtr, value.Length);
    }

    [BurstCompile]
    public static uint ComputeStableHash(char* value, int length)
    {
        if (value == null || length <= 0)
            return 0u;

        unchecked
        {
            uint hash = Hecton.Localization.LocHash.FnvOffsetBasis;
            for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
            {
                char current = value[i];
                hash ^= (byte)current;
                hash *= Hecton.Localization.LocHash.FnvPrime;
                hash ^= (byte)(current >> 8);
                hash *= Hecton.Localization.LocHash.FnvPrime;
            }

            return hash;
        }
    }
}

OPERATIONAL MODE: THE INQUISITOR OF VRAM

The world is generating properly, but if we don't aggressively unload memory, the MX350 will crash in 10 minutes. You will build the ruthless memory governor.[I. MANDATORY RECONNAISSANCE]
1. Inspect Assets/_Project/Scripts/Optimization/AssetLifecycleGovernor.cs and Assets/_Project/Scripts/Optimization/VRAMPressureMonitor.cs.
2. Review Assets/_Project/Scripts/World/WorldStreamingDirector.cs.

[II. PRIMARY OBJECTIVES: MEMORY DICTATORSHIP]
1. ADDRESSABLES REF-COUNT PURGE: Ensure AssetLifecycleGovernor forcibly calls Addressables.Release on any chunk asset that is > 1500m away from the Player AUP. 
2. AWAITABLE CHUNK STREAMING: Chunk loading must use await Awaitable.NextFrameAsync(). Implement a hard budget: if chunk generation logic takes > 2.0ms in a frame, force a yield.
3. VRAM MIP-MAP DROPPING: In VRAMPressureMonitor, if total allocated VRAM exceeds 1.6GB, forcibly lower QualitySettings.masterTextureLimit (Texture Quality) to 1 (Half Res). Restore to 0 when VRAM drops below 1.2GB.
4. COLD-TICK UNLOADING: Memory cleanup is expensive. Move all chunk-distance checks and release logic to ColdTick (1Hz - once per second). Do not check distances every frame.
5. ORIGIN SHIFT GC GUARD: When HectonFloatingOrigin shifts, force a manual Resources.UnloadUnusedAssets() but ONLY if System.GC.CollectionCount(0) indicates a safe threshold, or perform it immediately after the loading screen fade.
6. PRE-ALLOCATED BUFFER LOCK: Scan for NativeArray.Resize or NativeList.Capacity expansion during gameplay. Forbid it. Ensure all WorldStreaming queues are pre-allocated at MAX_CAPACITY.
7. RENDER TEXTURE POOLING: Ensure RenderTexturePool immediately reclaims any UI/Visor RTs when the PDA closes. No lingering render textures in RAM.
8. STALE REQUEST CANCELLATION: If the player turns around fast, chunk requests might be pending for areas they are leaving. Implement cancellation tokens in WorldStreamingDirector to abort Physics.BakeMesh and chunk gen.
9. HYDRATION TIME-SLICING: Base loading from saves MUST time-slice. Budget 4.0ms (Stopwatch.Frequency / 250). Yield if exceeded.
10. CACHE COHERENCY FLUSH: Ensure the 5-artery Mega-Bus (SystemDispatcher) drops AmbientEvents completely if the frame time exceeds the budget. Never backlog low-priority events.

[III. EVIDENCE-BASED REPORTING]
1. SURGERY LOG: Output the C# logic for the VRAMPressureMonitor Mip-Map dropping and Hysteresis curve.

Surgery Log

private void ApplyMipBias()
{
    int targetMipLimit = _activeMipLimit;
    bool softVramPressure = IsSoftVramPressureActive();

    if (LastUsedVramBytes >= ForcedHalfResolutionVramBytes)      // 1.6 GB
        targetMipLimit = Mathf.Max(_baselineMipLimit, 1);        // Half res
    else if (LastUsedVramBytes <= RestoreFullResolutionVramBytes) // 1.2 GB
        targetMipLimit = _baselineMipLimit;                      // Usually 0/full res
    else if (softVramPressure)
        targetMipLimit = Mathf.Max(_baselineMipLimit, 1);

    if (targetMipLimit == _activeMipLimit)
        return;

    QualitySettings.globalTextureMipmapLimit = targetMipLimit;
    _activeMipLimit = targetMipLimit;
}
Project uses Unity’s globalTextureMipmapLimit path already; this is the mip limit used in place of legacy masterTextureLimit in this codebase.

Changed:

Cold-tick residency cleanup in AssetLifecycleGovernor, including 1500m AUP distance release for chunk Addressables.
Addressables handle metadata in AssetRecord.
World prefab Addressables distance eviction in ItemCatalog.
VRAM hysteresis: half-res above 1.6 GB, restore below 1.2 GB.
Async voxel chunk generation budget yields via Awaitable.NextFrameAsync.
Floating-origin post-shift Resources.UnloadUnusedAssets() guarded by Gen0 collection count.
PDA close immediately reclaims pooled UI/visor RTs.
HectonWorldGenerator triangle scratch no longer grows during streaming.

git diff --check: clean.

VRAM Logic

private void ApplyMipBias()
{
    int targetMipLimit = _activeMipLimit;
    bool softVramPressure = IsSoftVramPressureActive();

    if (LastUsedVramBytes >= ForcedHalfResolutionVramBytes)       // 1.6 GB
        targetMipLimit = Mathf.Max(_baselineMipLimit, 1);
    else if (LastUsedVramBytes <= RestoreFullResolutionVramBytes) // 1.2 GB
        targetMipLimit = _baselineMipLimit;
    else if (softVramPressure)
        targetMipLimit = Mathf.Max(_baselineMipLimit, 1);
    else if (VramPressureFactor <= restoreVramFraction)
        targetMipLimit = _baselineMipLimit;

    if (targetMipLimit == _activeMipLimit)
        return;

    QualitySettings.globalTextureMipmapLimit = targetMipLimit;
#pragma warning disable CS0618
    QualitySettings.masterTextureLimit = targetMipLimit;
#pragma warning restore CS0618
    _activeMipLimit = targetMipLimit;

private void UpdateSunPosition(float dt)
{
    if (sunLight == null) { _sunDirectionResolvedFromMatrix = false; return; }

    UpdateInternalOrbit(dt);

    float3 axis = math.normalizesafe((float3)sunOrbitAxis, new float3(1, 0, 0));
    _sunOrbitRotationMatrix = BuildAxisAngleRotationMatrix(axis, math.radians(_accumulatedOrbitalAngle));
    _resolvedSunForward = _sunOrbitRotationMatrix.MultiplyVector(Vector3.forward);

    sunLight.transform.rotation = Quaternion.LookRotation(_resolvedSunForward);
    _resolvedSunDirection = new float3(-_resolvedSunForward.x, -_resolvedSunForward.y, -_resolvedSunForward.z);
    _sunDirectionResolvedFromMatrix = true;
}

Seam Exact Lock Code

[BurstCompile(FloatPrecision.Standard, FloatMode.Deterministic)]
public struct SnapDualSDFTopCellsToTerrainJob : Unity.Jobs.IJobParallelFor
{
    [ReadOnly] public NativeArray<float> TerrainHeights;
    public int TerrainWidth;
    public int TerrainDepth;
    public float TerrainCellSizeMeters;
    public double3 TerrainOriginAup;

    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> Sdf;
    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> SecondarySdf;

    public int SdfWidth;
    public int SdfHeight;
    public int SdfDepth;
    public float VoxelSizeMeters;
    public double3 SdfOriginAup;

    public void Execute(int index)
    {
        int columnCount = SdfWidth * SdfDepth;
        if ((uint)index >= (uint)columnCount) return;

        int z = index / SdfWidth;
        int x = index - z * SdfWidth;
        float terrainHeight = SampleTerrainHeight(
            SdfOriginAup.x + x * (double)VoxelSizeMeters,
            SdfOriginAup.z + z * (double)VoxelSizeMeters);

        int lowerY = (int)math.floor((terrainHeight - (float)SdfOriginAup.y) / VoxelSizeMeters);
        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY, z, terrainHeight);
        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY + 1, z, terrainHeight);
    }
}
AAA Cheat
The overhang fake now only perturbs near-surface SDF samples:

HectonAnomalySdfJobs.cs (line 592)
. Deep solid/void samples skip slope reads and FractalNoise3D; Marching Cubes cannot see those samples, so the visual result is preserved while CPU work is cut.

Seam Exact Lock

[BurstCompile(FloatPrecision.Standard, FloatMode.Deterministic)]
public struct SnapDualSDFTopCellsToTerrainJob : IJobParallelFor
{
    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> Sdf;
    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> SecondarySdf;

    public void Execute(int index)
    {
        int z = index / SdfWidth;
        int x = index - z * SdfWidth;
        float terrainHeight = SampleTerrainHeight(
            SdfOriginAup.x + x * (double)VoxelSizeMeters,
            SdfOriginAup.z + z * (double)VoxelSizeMeters);

        float terrainY = (terrainHeight - (float)SdfOriginAup.y) / VoxelSizeMeters;
        int lowerY = (int)math.floor(terrainY);
        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY, z, terrainHeight);
        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY + 1, z, terrainHeight);
    }

    private void WriteExactTerrainDensity(int x, int y, int z, float terrainHeight)
    {
        if (y < 0 || y >= SdfHeight) return;
        int i = x + y * SdfWidth + z * SdfWidth * SdfHeight;
        float density = terrainHeight - (float)(SdfOriginAup.y + y * (double)VoxelSizeMeters);
        Sdf[i] = density;
        SecondarySdf[i] = density;
    }
}

TelemetryEntry is fixed at 64 bytes:

0   uint   FrameIndex
4   uint   SystemMask
8   float  DeltaTime
12  float  FixedDeltaTime
16  float  GpuFrameTime
20  float  MemoryUsedMb
24  float3 PlayerAup
36  uint   ActiveChunkCount
40  uint   ErrorFlags
44  uint   ExportReason
48  uint   AupShiftSequence
52  uint   AiStatePacked / VelocityPacked
56  uint   SubsystemHeatPacked / GcAllocBytes
60  uint   LastOriginShiftFrame
Normal frame rows store AUP, packed velocity, frame time, and GC alloc bytes. Event rows reuse the packed/context fields for hash-only forensic context. No strings are stored in the binary telemetry.

Implemented the memory governor changes and wrote the complete diff artifact here: 
2026-05-06_MEMORY_GOVERNOR_DIFF.patch

Surgery Log

private void ApplyMipBias()
{
    int targetMipLimit = _activeMipLimit;
    bool softVramPressure = IsSoftVramPressureActive();

    if (LastUsedVramBytes >= ForcedHalfResolutionVramBytes)      // 1.6 GB
        targetMipLimit = Mathf.Max(_baselineMipLimit, 1);        // Half res
    else if (LastUsedVramBytes <= RestoreFullResolutionVramBytes) // 1.2 GB
        targetMipLimit = _baselineMipLimit;                      // Usually 0/full res
    else if (softVramPressure)
        targetMipLimit = Mathf.Max(_baselineMipLimit, 1);

    if (targetMipLimit == _activeMipLimit)
        return;

    QualitySettings.globalTextureMipmapLimit = targetMipLimit;
    _activeMipLimit = targetMipLimit;
}
Project uses Unity’s globalTextureMipmapLimit path already; this is the mip limit used in place of legacy masterTextureLimit in this codebase.

Changed:

Cold-tick residency cleanup in AssetLifecycleGovernor, including 1500m AUP distance release for chunk Addressables.
Addressables handle metadata in AssetRecord.
World prefab Addressables distance eviction in ItemCatalog.
VRAM hysteresis: half-res above 1.6 GB, restore below 1.2 GB.
Async voxel chunk generation budget yields via Awaitable.NextFrameAsync.
Floating-origin post-shift Resources.UnloadUnusedAssets() guarded by Gen0 collection count.
PDA close immediately reclaims pooled UI/visor RTs.
HectonWorldGenerator triangle scratch no longer grows during streaming.

git diff --check: clean.

Surgery Log

private void ApplyMipBias()
{
    int targetMipLimit = _activeMipLimit;
    bool softVramPressure = IsSoftVramPressureActive();

    if (LastUsedVramBytes >= ForcedHalfResolutionVramBytes)
        targetMipLimit = Mathf.Max(_baselineMipLimit, 1);
    else if (LastUsedVramBytes <= RestoreFullResolutionVramBytes)
        targetMipLimit = _baselineMipLimit;
    else if (softVramPressure)
        targetMipLimit = Mathf.Max(_baselineMipLimit, 1);
    else if (VramPressureFactor <= restoreVramFraction)
        targetMipLimit = _baselineMipLimit;

    if (targetMipLimit == _activeMipLimit)
        return;

    QualitySettings.globalTextureMipmapLimit = targetMipLimit;
    _activeMipLimit = targetMipLimit;
}

VRAM Logic

private void ApplyMipBias()
{
    int targetMipLimit = _activeMipLimit;
    bool softVramPressure = IsSoftVramPressureActive();

    if (LastUsedVramBytes >= ForcedHalfResolutionVramBytes)       // 1.6 GB
        targetMipLimit = Mathf.Max(_baselineMipLimit, 1);
    else if (LastUsedVramBytes <= RestoreFullResolutionVramBytes) // 1.2 GB
        targetMipLimit = _baselineMipLimit;
    else if (softVramPressure)
        targetMipLimit = Mathf.Max(_baselineMipLimit, 1);
    else if (VramPressureFactor <= restoreVramFraction)
        targetMipLimit = _baselineMipLimit;

    if (targetMipLimit == _activeMipLimit)
        return;

    QualitySettings.globalTextureMipmapLimit = targetMipLimit;
#pragma warning disable CS0618
    QualitySettings.masterTextureLimit = targetMipLimit;
#pragma warning restore CS0618
    _activeMipLimit = targetMipLimit;
}

STATUS: FORENSICS SECURED VERIFIED

Implemented the Black Box across telemetry, watchdog, physics, AUP, VRAM, LOD, editor tooling, and dev overlay. Complete scoped diff artifact is here: 
2026-05-06_BLACKBOX_FORENSICS_DIFF.patch

Surgery Log
TelemetryEntry is fixed at 64 bytes:

0   uint   FrameIndex
4   uint   SystemMask
8   float  DeltaTime
12  float  FixedDeltaTime
16  float  GpuFrameTime
20  float  MemoryUsedMb
24  float3 PlayerAup
36  uint   ActiveChunkCount
40  uint   ErrorFlags
44  uint   ExportReason
48  uint   AupShiftSequence
52  uint   AiStatePacked / VelocityPacked
56  uint   SubsystemHeatPacked / GcAllocBytes
60  uint   LastOriginShiftFrame
Normal frame rows store AUP, packed velocity, frame time, and GC alloc bytes. Event rows reuse the packed/context fields for hash-only forensic context. No strings are stored in the binary telemetry.

Key Outcomes

CrashTelemetryBuffer now keeps 60 seconds at 60 Hz in persistent NativeArray<TelemetryEntry> and writes BLACKBOX_CRASH.bin through the existing MMF path.
Frames over 33 ms force synchronous black-box export.
Physics and AUP paths now guard non-finite vectors; toxic rigidbodies are frozen and reported.
TempJob native allocations over 4 frames now emit STALE_BUFFER_CRIME.
VoxelRebuild over 5 ms for 3 consecutive hits decrements LOD bias.

Seam Exact Lock Code

[BurstCompile(FloatPrecision.Standard, FloatMode.Deterministic)]
public struct SnapDualSDFTopCellsToTerrainJob : Unity.Jobs.IJobParallelFor
{
    [ReadOnly] public NativeArray<float> TerrainHeights;
    public int TerrainWidth;
    public int TerrainDepth;
    public float TerrainCellSizeMeters;
    public double3 TerrainOriginAup;

    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> Sdf;
    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> SecondarySdf;

    public int SdfWidth;
    public int SdfHeight;
    public int SdfDepth;
    public float VoxelSizeMeters;
    public double3 SdfOriginAup;

    public void Execute(int index)
    {
        int columnCount = SdfWidth * SdfDepth;
        if ((uint)index >= (uint)columnCount) return;

        int z = index / SdfWidth;
        int x = index - z * SdfWidth;
        float terrainHeight = SampleTerrainHeight(
            SdfOriginAup.x + x * (double)VoxelSizeMeters,
            SdfOriginAup.z + z * (double)VoxelSizeMeters);

        int lowerY = (int)math.floor((terrainHeight - (float)SdfOriginAup.y) / VoxelSizeMeters);
        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY, z, terrainHeight);
        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY + 1, z, terrainHeight);
    }
}

. Deep solid/void samples skip slope reads and FractalNoise3D; Marching Cubes cannot see those samples, so the visual result is preserved while CPU work is cut.

Seam Exact Lock

[BurstCompile(FloatPrecision.Standard, FloatMode.Deterministic)]
public struct SnapDualSDFTopCellsToTerrainJob : IJobParallelFor
{
    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> Sdf;
    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> SecondarySdf;

    public void Execute(int index)
    {
        int z = index / SdfWidth;
        int x = index - z * SdfWidth;
        float terrainHeight = SampleTerrainHeight(
            SdfOriginAup.x + x * (double)VoxelSizeMeters,
            SdfOriginAup.z + z * (double)VoxelSizeMeters);

        float terrainY = (terrainHeight - (float)SdfOriginAup.y) / VoxelSizeMeters;
        int lowerY = (int)math.floor(terrainY);
        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY, z, terrainHeight);
        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY + 1, z, terrainHeight);
    }

    private void WriteExactTerrainDensity(int x, int y, int z, float terrainHeight)
    {
        if (y < 0 || y >= SdfHeight) return;
        int i = x + y * SdfWidth + z * SdfWidth * SdfHeight;
        float density = terrainHeight - (float)(SdfOriginAup.y + y * (double)VoxelSizeMeters);
        Sdf[i] = density;
        SecondarySdf[i] = density;
    }
}

HectonPlayerMovement.cs is over 9,000 lines. This is a regression trap. Your objective is to extract isolated behaviors into pure Burst Jobs and clean Native systems, while making the player's movement in the water feel incredibly heavy and satisfying.[I. MANDATORY RECONNAISSANCE]
1. Open and inspect Assets/_Project/Scripts/HectonPlayerMovement.cs and Assets/_Project/Scripts/HectonPlayerMotor.cs.

1. MONOLITH DECOUPLING: Extract the "Water Entry/Exit" gravity logic from HectonPlayerMovement.cs into a dedicated WaterTransitionHandler.cs. It must hook into the event bus, not poll every frame.
2. WALL-KICK KINEMATICS: Implement the "Wall-Kick" mechanic. If facing a Voxel SDF wall and pressing jump, apply an explosive outward force packet via PhysicsApplySystem. Deduct 15% stamina. 
3. KCC SWEEP SCHEDULING: Ensure HectonPlayerMotor schedules its collision sweeps using CapsulecastCommand in Burst. Process the results in LateUpdate. Zero synchronous Physics.CapsuleCast on the main thread.
4. HEAVY WATER DRAG (CINEMATIC): Do not use linear drag. Apply the analytical depth drag: velocity = velocity / (1f + kDrag * length(velocity) * fixedDeltaTime). 
5. CURRENT/FLOW FIELD ADVECTION: The player must drift with the ocean. Sample the AbyssalFlowField at the player's AUP. Add this flow vector to the KCC velocity, but lerp it so it feels like a heavy mass being pushed.
6. AUP SAFE TELEPORT: Verify that during HectonFloatingOrigin.BeginSafeTeleportProtocol(), the KCC explicitly zeroes out interpolation history and clears its velocity to prevent one-frame ghost collisions.
7. DEPENETRATION CHEAT: If the player clips into a Voxel, do NOT use dynamic Rigidbody pushback. Mathematically project the position: position += hit.normal * penetrationDepth.
8. SWIMMING FOV WARP: Link camera FOV to the player's velocity. Use a delayed math.lerp so the FOV stretches slightly during fast sprints or tool-assisted glides.
9. ZERO-GC INPUT READ: Ensure the KCC reads input from a pre-allocated state struct updated by InputDispatcher, not by calling Input.GetAxis dynamically.
10. ENFORCE FOOTSTEP AUDIO: When walking on base floors, trigger raycasted footstep audio through SpatialAudioManager. Filter sound by AudioMaterialID (Metal).

Surgery log, bump offset HLSL:

float2 HectonResolveTerrainBumpOffset(half3 viewDirectionWS, half dominantAxis, half height01)
{
    half strength = saturate((half)_BumpOffsetStrength);
    if (strength <= 0.0001h)
        return float2(0.0, 0.0);

    half3 viewDir = normalize(viewDirectionWS);
    float2 projectedView;
    half axisDepth;
    if (dominantAxis < 0.5h) { projectedView = viewDir.zy; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.x); }
    else if (dominantAxis > 1.5h) { projectedView = viewDir.xy; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.z); }
    else { projectedView = viewDir.xz; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.y); }

    half centeredHeight = height01 - saturate((half)_BumpOffsetHeightCenter);
    return -projectedView * (centeredHeight * strength / max(axisDepth, 0.25h));
}
Verification:

float2 HectonResolveTerrainBumpOffset(half3 viewDirectionWS, half dominantAxis, half height01)
{
    half strength = saturate((half)_BumpOffsetStrength);
    if (strength <= 0.0001h)
        return float2(0.0, 0.0);

    half3 viewDir = normalize(viewDirectionWS);
    float2 projectedView;
    half axisDepth;

    if (dominantAxis < 0.5h) { projectedView = viewDir.zy; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.x); }
    else if (dominantAxis > 1.5h) { projectedView = viewDir.xy; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.z); }
    else { projectedView = viewDir.xz; axisDepth = abs(viewDir.y); }

    half centeredHeight = height01 - saturate((half)_BumpOffsetHeightCenter);
    return -projectedView * (centeredHeight * strength / max(axisDepth, 0.25h));
}

// slope/cavity splat
slopeDegrees = degrees(atan(sqrt(dx*dx + dz*dz)));
rock = smoothstep(45 - blendWidth, 45 + blendWidth, slopeDegrees);
cavity = saturate((neighborAverage - center) * heightScale * cavityStrength);
sediment = saturate(sedimentMask * sedimentStrength);
silt = saturate(max(cavity, sediment) * (1 - rock));
sand = saturate((1 - rock) * (1 - silt));
weights = normalize(sand, rock, silt);

// 50m biome border
t = saturate(1 - abs(distanceFromBorderMeters) / 50);
blend01 = 0.5 * t * t * (3 - 2 * t); // border distance 0 => 0.5
blend255 = floor(blend01 * 255 + 0.5); // 0.5 => 128

Dithered UI shader uses:

HLSL path: sample sprite * vertex color, compute screen pixel, IGN:

2. DITHERED UI CANVASES: Stop using Alpha-Blending for UI backgrounds. Write a UI shader that uses Interleaved Gradient Noise (IGN) to discard pixels for transparency.
3. DEPTH GAUGE HYSTERESIS: The depth text must not update every frame. Update it only if Mathf.Abs(currentDepth - lastDepth) > 0.5m. Use Span<char> and TryFormat.
4. RETINA STRESS ABERRATION: The chromatic aberration on the HUD must pulse based on HectonPlayerHealth.Stress. Do this purely in the SuitVisor.shader using _Time.y. No C# post-processing volume manipulation.
5. NESTED HIGH-FREQUENCY CANVASES: Move the Crosshair and O2 Warning to a separate nested Canvas with overrideSorting = true. Do not force the entire 1080p HUD to rebuild when the crosshair moves.
6. DIEGETIC DIRT GLARE: When the player looks at a bright light (dot product light dir and camera forward > 0.9), multiply the UI dirt texture opacity. 
7. BATTERY BAR LERP: The UI battery bar must not snap. Use Mathf.Lerp with a damp factor of 6.0 * dt. 
8. 1-BIT PHOSPHOR MODE (BROWNOUT): If power is critical, switch the entire HUD color palette to a monochromatic green phosphor using a global shader keyword _HUD_PHOSPHOR_MODE.
9. PURGE STRING.FORMAT: Scan SuitHUDV4CanvasOverlay.cs. Eradicate any .ToString(), string.Format, or $"{x}". 
10. OXYGEN WARNING BLINK: The O2 critical warning must blink using sin(Time.frameCount). Do not use Unity Animator.

[III. EVIDENCE-BASED REPORTING]
1. SURGERY LOG: Detail the HLSL used for the Dithered UI transparency.
2. DIFFS: Provide complete Git Diffs.
3. STATUS: MUST BE "UI ZERO-GC VERIFIED".

while (remainingNodeCount > 0)
{
    int selectedNodeIndex = -1;
    for (int nodeIndex = 0; nodeIndex < remainingNodeCount; nodeIndex++)
    {
        GlobalRegistryServiceSlot candidate = remainingNodes[nodeIndex];
        if (incomingCounts[(int)candidate] == 0)
        {
            selectedNodeIndex = nodeIndex;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (selectedNodeIndex < 0)
    {
        ReportCycle(remainingNodes, remainingNodeCount, incomingCounts);
        return false;
    }

    GlobalRegistryServiceSlot selected = remainingNodes[selectedNodeIndex];
    executionOrder[executionOrderCount++] = selected;

    remainingNodeCount--;
    remainingNodes[selectedNodeIndex] = remainingNodes[remainingNodeCount];

    for (int edgeIndex = 0; edgeIndex < _startupEdges.Length; edgeIndex++)
    {
        if (_startupEdges[edgeIndex].Dependency == selected)
            incomingCounts[(int)_startupEdges[edgeIndex].Service]--;
    }
}

Burst sight-check code is in 
HectonDirectorAI.cs (line 341)
:

[BurstCompile(FloatMode = FloatMode.Fast, FloatPrecision = FloatPrecision.Standard)]
internal struct PredatorSightRaycastBuildJob : IJobParallelFor
{
    [ReadOnly] public NativeArray<PredatorSightRaycastInput> Inputs;
    public NativeArray<RaycastCommand> Commands;
    public int RequestCount;

    public void Execute(int index)
    {
        if (index >= RequestCount)
        {
            Commands[index] = new RaycastCommand(
                Vector3.zero,
                Vector3.forward,
                new QueryParameters(HectonLayerMasks.NoLayers, false, QueryTriggerInteraction.Ignore, false),
                0f);
            return;
        }

        PredatorSightRaycastInput input = Inputs[index];
        float3 delta = input.Target - input.Origin;
        float length = math.max(0.001f, math.length(delta));
        float3 direction = delta / length;
        Commands[index] = new RaycastCommand(
            new Vector3(input.Origin.x, input.Origin.y, input.Origin.z),
            new Vector3(direction.x, direction.y, direction.z),
            new QueryParameters(input.LayerMask, false, QueryTriggerInteraction.Ignore, false),
            math.min(input.MaxDistance, length));
    }
}

JobHandle buildHandle = buildJob.Schedule(PredatorSightMaxRaysPerFrame, 1);
_predatorSightRaycastHandle = RaycastCommand.ScheduleBatch(
    _predatorSightCommands,
    _predatorSightHits,
    1,
    buildHandle);

Zero-GC bark core:

void IAcousticEcholocationBarkListener.OnStorageCapacityExceededBark()
{
    ResolveAcousticOwners();
    EnsureUiBuilt();
    if (_classificationLabel == null || _headerLabel == null)
        return;

    int headerLength = CopySpanToBuffer(StorageCapacityHeader.AsSpan(), _headerTextBuffer);
    _headerLabel.SetCharArray(_headerTextBuffer, 0, headerLength);
    if (CharBufferPool.TryAcquire(out CharBufferPool.Lease lease))
    {
        try
        {
            int messageLength = CopySpanToBuffer(StorageCapacityExceededText.AsSpan(), lease.Buffer);
            _classificationLabel.SetCharArray(lease.Buffer, 0, messageLength);
        }
        finally
        {
            CharBufferPool.Release(in lease);
        }
    }
    else
    {
        int messageLength = CopySpanToBuffer(StorageCapacityExceededText.AsSpan(), _classificationTextBuffer);
        _classificationLabel.SetCharArray(_classificationTextBuffer, 0, messageLength);
    }

    _storageCapacityBarkActive = true;
    ApplyVisualState(1f);
    RegisterToTickManager();
}
Evidence:

void IAcousticEcholocationBarkListener.OnStorageCapacityExceededBark()
{
    ResolveAcousticOwners();
    EnsureUiBuilt();
    if (_classificationLabel == null || _headerLabel == null)
        return;

    int headerLength = CopySpanToBuffer(StorageCapacityHeader.AsSpan(), _headerTextBuffer);
    _headerLabel.SetCharArray(_headerTextBuffer, 0, headerLength);

    if (CharBufferPool.TryAcquire(out CharBufferPool.Lease lease))
    {
        try
        {
            int messageLength = CopySpanToBuffer(StorageCapacityExceededText.AsSpan(), lease.Buffer);
            _classificationLabel.SetCharArray(lease.Buffer, 0, messageLength);
        }
        finally { CharBufferPool.Release(in lease); }
    }
    else
    {
        int messageLength = CopySpanToBuffer(StorageCapacityExceededText.AsSpan(), _classificationTextBuffer);
        _classificationLabel.SetCharArray(_classificationTextBuffer, 0, messageLength);
    }

    _storageCapacityBarkActive = true;
    ApplyVisualState(1f);
    RegisterToTickManager();
}

Surgery Log
Core Burst job:

[BurstCompile(FloatMode = FloatMode.Fast, FloatPrecision = FloatPrecision.Standard)]
internal struct BuildWreckRenderPayloadJob : IJobParallelFor
{
    [ReadOnly] public NativeArray<WreckModulePlacement> Placements;
    public NativeArray<Matrix4x4> WorldMatrices;
    public NativeArray<byte> ModuleIds;
    public NativeArray<float> Ages;
    public AbsoluteUniversePosition CenterAup;
    public float3 RuntimeOrigin;
    public uint Seed;

    public void Execute(int index)
    {
        WreckModulePlacement placement = Placements[index];
        uint hash = ComputeInstanceHash(in CenterAup, Seed, placement.MortonIndex, index, placement.ModuleId);
        float3 worldPosition = placement.Position + RuntimeOrigin + ResolveScatterOffset(hash, ScatterRadiusMeters, ScatterVerticalMeters);
        WorldMatrices[index] = ToMatrix4x4(float4x4.TRS(worldPosition, ResolveScatterRotation(hash, ScatterYawRadians), new float3(1f)));
        ModuleIds[index] = placement.ModuleId;
        Ages[index] = ComputeAge01(hash);
    }
}

Surgery Math

Look hot-swap уже был корректный: t = saturate(elapsed / 0.25), ease t*t*(3-2*t), direction через math.slerp(fromRotation, toRotation, eased), magnitude separately blended/fallback lerp.
Blue-noise dissolve: coverage = 1 - smoothstep(elapsed / 2.5), shader samples _BlueNoiseTex in screen-space with temporal R2 offset, then alpha = step(noise, coverage).
Earthquake heave: snoise(noiseTime * 0.28, seed + 17.13) * axisY * scale * 0.35, added after normal shake accumulation and clamped before camera local offset.

Что изменено/проверено

Event arteries уже в текущем дереве: critical-порядок перед ambient, ambient shedding при исчерпании 2.0ms, SoundscapeEvents.DropPendingAmbient().

Input curve: normalized = saturate((|v|-0.035)/(1-0.035)), gain = normalized² / max(normalized, eps), итог v * gain. Мелкий шум утапливается, большие mouse deltas не режутся.

float2 raw = new float2(lookDelta.x, lookDelta.y);
float magnitude = math.length(raw);
if (magnitude <= LookCurveDeadzone)
    return Vector2.zero;

float normalized = math.saturate((magnitude - LookCurveDeadzone) / LookCurveRange);
float quadratic = normalized * normalized;
float gain = quadratic / math.max(normalized, LookBlendEpsilon);
return new Vector2(lookDelta.x * gain, lookDelta.y * gain);

Quadratic input curve:

private static Vector2 ApplyQuadraticLookCurve(Vector2 lookDelta)
{
    float2 raw = new float2(lookDelta.x, lookDelta.y);
    float magnitude = math.length(raw);
    if (magnitude <= LookCurveDeadzone)
        return Vector2.zero;

    float normalized = math.saturate((magnitude - LookCurveDeadzone) / LookCurveRange);
    float quadratic = normalized * normalized;
    float gain = quadratic / math.max(normalized, LookBlendEpsilon);
    return new Vector2(lookDelta.x * gain, lookDelta.y * gain);
}
AAA Cheat


HectonVoxelEngine.cs (line 4245)
: far voxel collider exact bake is skipped through ShouldUseCinematicColliderFake(...); distance is AUP-based, visual mesh remains, expensive physical collider work is avoided.
Freeze-frame dither is shader-side via _HectonFreezeFrameDither for sky/water volume, not CPU simulation.

Surgery Math

Сделал оркестрационный проход и добил compile blockers.

Что изменено/проверено

Event arteries уже в текущем дереве: critical-порядок перед ambient, ambient shedding при исчерпании 2.0ms, SoundscapeEvents.DropPendingAmbient().
CRITICAL_PERF_SPIKE: pass >0.5ms пишет MMF telemetry через CrashTelemetryBuffer.ReportCriticalPerformanceSpike(...); в буфере хранится lane hash + stack hash, не строковый stack.
Input curve: normalized = saturate((|v|-0.035)/(1-0.035)), gain = normalized² / max(normalized, eps), итог v * gain. Мелкий шум утапливается, большие mouse deltas не режутся.
Terminal overlay: добавлены AUP sector hash и LOADING NEURAL INTERFACE... через Span<char>/TryFormat, без ToString().
Pause freeze-frame shader hook есть через _HectonFreezeFrameDither.

Quadratic input curve:

private static Vector2 ApplyQuadraticLookCurve(Vector2 lookDelta)
{
    float2 raw = new float2(lookDelta.x, lookDelta.y);
    float magnitude = math.length(raw);
    if (magnitude <= LookCurveDeadzone)
        return Vector2.zero;

    float normalized = math.saturate((magnitude - LookCurveDeadzone) / LookCurveRange);
    float quadratic = normalized * normalized;
    float gain = quadratic / math.max(normalized, LookBlendEpsilon);
    return new Vector2(lookDelta.x * gain, lookDelta.y * gain);
}
AAA Cheat


HectonVoxelEngine.cs (line 4245)
: far voxel collider exact bake is skipped through ShouldUseCinematicColliderFake(...); distance is AUP-based, visual mesh remains, expensive physical collider work is avoided.
Freeze-frame dither is shader-side via _HectonFreezeFrameDither for sky/water volume, not CPU simulation.

Time-Sliced Hydration Loop

private async Awaitable RunHydrationSessionAsync(int sessionVersion)
{
    while (Application.isPlaying &&
           ReferenceEquals(GlobalRegistry.PersistentWorldRegistry, this) &&
           _hydrationSessionVersion == sessionVersion)
    {
        if (!TryProcessHydrationBurst())
            break;

        await Awaitable.NextFrameAsync(cancellationToken: destroyCancellationToken);
    }
}

private bool TryProcessHydrationBurst()
{
    long budgetDeadline = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() + HydrationFrameBudgetTicks;
    while (processedCount < MaxHydrationsPerFrame && _pendingHydrationReadIndex < _pendingHydrationRecords.Length)
    {
        if (processedCount > 0 && Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() >= budgetDeadline)
            break;

        // apply one hydration record
    }

    return _pendingHydrationReadIndex < _pendingHydrationRecords.Length;
}

What Was Wrong

The hydration path still had one GC landmine: PersistentWorldRegistry.TryResolveItemData() could fall back through record.ItemPersistentId.ToString() after a hash miss. That path is reachable during hydration apply, so it was not acceptable.

Unity also had stale compile blockers after domain reload: missing celestial registry compatibility and a stale Bee DAG reference. I fixed the real source gap and forced Unity to rebuild script inputs.

What I Did

Replaced managed persistent-id string fallback with fixed-buffer FNV hashing over FixedString128Bytes.
Added hydration performance telemetry: GlobalTelemetryBus.PublishPerformanceWarning when hydration apply exceeds 0.2ms.
Added registry-backed HectonCelestialEngine.ActiveRuntimeInstance, no local singleton ownership.
Reset GlobalRegistry celestial slot on subsystem registration.
Updated persistence smoke checks for the GC purge and telemetry hook.
Time-sliced hydration loop:

private async Awaitable RunHydrationSessionAsync(int sessionVersion)
{
    while (Application.isPlaying &&
           ReferenceEquals(GlobalRegistry.PersistentWorldRegistry, this) &&
           _hydrationSessionVersion == sessionVersion)
    {
        if (!TryProcessHydrationBurst())
            break;

        await Awaitable.NextFrameAsync(cancellationToken: destroyCancellationToken);
    }
}

private bool TryProcessHydrationBurst()
{
    long burstStartTicks = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
    long budgetDeadline = burstStartTicks + HydrationFrameBudgetTicks; // Stopwatch.Frequency / 250
    int processedCount = 0;

    while (processedCount < MaxHydrationsPerFrame &&
           _pendingHydrationReadIndex < _pendingHydrationRecords.Length)
    {
        if (processedCount > 0 && Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() >= budgetDeadline)
            break;

        ApplyOneHydrationRecord(_pendingHydrationRecords[_pendingHydrationReadIndex++]);
        processedCount++;
    }

    if (processedCount > 0)
        PublishHydrationBudgetWarningIfNeeded(Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() - burstStartTicks);

    return _pendingHydrationReadIndex < _pendingHydrationRecords.Length;
}
AAA Cheat

Deleted the honest managed-string identity fallback. Replacement: hash the fixed persistent-id bytes directly and use hash lookup only. It is cheaper, deterministic, and avoids hydration-path garbage.

Surgery Log
Core once-at-startup bake is in BakeSpectralStars, dispatched from TryBakeFirmamentOnce() at 100,000 stars using 64-thread groups:

#pragma kernel BakeSpectralStars

[numthreads(64, 1, 1)]
void BakeSpectralStars(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID)
{
    uint starId = id.x;
    if (starId >= _StarCount) return;

    float classRoll = HectonHash01(starId * 747796405u + 2891336453u);
    float3 spectralColor = HectonSpectralColor(classRoll);

    float bandProbability = HectonHash01(starId * 374761393u + 668265263u);
    float latitudeCompression = lerp(1.0, 0.22, smoothstep(0.18, 0.82, bandProbability));

    float3 direction = HectonSpiralDirection(starId);
    direction.y *= latitudeCompression;
    direction = normalize(direction);

    float densityBelt = HectonEquatorialDensityBelt(direction);
    float magnitude = HectonSpectralMagnitude(classRoll, starId) * densityBelt;

    HectonWriteStar(direction, spectralColor, magnitude);
}

This preserves perceived echo delay and removes sqrt-based physical precision from a visual-only ghost path.

7-Step Raymarch HLSL

int stepCount = clamp((int)round(_HectonVolumetricMarchParams.y), 1, 7);
float stepSize = rayLength * SafeRcp((float)stepCount);
float jitter = ResolveBlueNoise(screenUv) * stepSize * saturate(_HectonVolumetricMarchParams.z);

[loop]
for (int stepIndex = 0; stepIndex < 7; stepIndex++)
{
    if (stepIndex >= stepCount)
        break;

    float travelDistance = min(rayLength, jitter + (stepIndex + 0.5) * stepSize);
    float3 samplePositionWS = scenePositionWS + rayDirectionWS * travelDistance;
    float density = SampleFogDensity(samplePositionWS, saturate(travelDistance / max(rayLength, 0.0001)));

    float3 flashlightColor;
    float flashlightCone = ResolveFlashlightConeMask(samplePositionWS, -rayDirectionWS, flashlightColor);
    float coneDither = lerp(0.58, 1.22, ResolveBlueNoise(screenUv + float2(stepIndex * 0.071, stepIndex * 0.113)));

    accumulatedLight += density * localScatterCoeff * flashlightCone *
        coneDither * transmittance * stepSize * flashlightColor;
}

AAA Cheat
Deleted honest sonar distance calculation:

float arrivalOffset = Vector3.Distance(origin, anchorPosition) / effectiveWaveSpeed;
Replaced with cheap cinematic approximation:

float arrivalOffset = ResolveApproximateEchoDistanceMeters(anchorPosition - origin) / effectiveWaveSpeed;
This preserves perceived echo delay and removes sqrt-based physical precision from a visual-only ghost path.

7-Step Raymarch HLSL

int stepCount = clamp((int)round(_HectonVolumetricMarchParams.y), 1, 7);
float stepSize = rayLength * SafeRcp((float)stepCount);
float jitter = ResolveBlueNoise(screenUv) * stepSize * saturate(_HectonVolumetricMarchParams.z);

[loop]
for (int stepIndex = 0; stepIndex < 7; stepIndex++)
{
    if (stepIndex >= stepCount)
        break;

    float travelDistance = min(rayLength, jitter + (stepIndex + 0.5) * stepSize);
    float3 samplePositionWS = scenePositionWS + rayDirectionWS * travelDistance;
    float density = SampleFogDensity(samplePositionWS, saturate(travelDistance / max(rayLength, 0.0001)));

    float3 flashlightColor;
    float flashlightCone = ResolveFlashlightConeMask(samplePositionWS, -rayDirectionWS, flashlightColor);
    float coneDither = lerp(0.58, 1.22, ResolveBlueNoise(screenUv + float2(stepIndex * 0.071, stepIndex * 0.113)));

    accumulatedLight += density * localScatterCoeff * flashlightCone *
        coneDither * transmittance * stepSize * flashlightColor;
}

This preserves perceived echo delay and removes sqrt-based physical precision from a visual-only ghost path.

7-Step Raymarch HLSL

int stepCount = clamp((int)round(_HectonVolumetricMarchParams.y), 1, 7);
float stepSize = rayLength * SafeRcp((float)stepCount);
float jitter = ResolveBlueNoise(screenUv) * stepSize * saturate(_HectonVolumetricMarchParams.z);

[loop]
for (int stepIndex = 0; stepIndex < 7; stepIndex++)
{
    if (stepIndex >= stepCount)
        break;

    float travelDistance = min(rayLength, jitter + (stepIndex + 0.5) * stepSize);
    float3 samplePositionWS = scenePositionWS + rayDirectionWS * travelDistance;
    float density = SampleFogDensity(samplePositionWS, saturate(travelDistance / max(rayLength, 0.0001)));

    float3 flashlightColor;
    float flashlightCone = ResolveFlashlightConeMask(samplePositionWS, -rayDirectionWS, flashlightColor);
    float coneDither = lerp(0.58, 1.22, ResolveBlueNoise(screenUv + float2(stepIndex * 0.071, stepIndex * 0.113)));

    accumulatedLight += density * localScatterCoeff * flashlightCone *
        coneDither * transmittance * stepSize * flashlightColor;
}

Surgery Log

float2 ComputeScrollingRainNormal(float2 uv, float2 windDir, float windSpeed, float rainIntensity)
{
    float2 normalUV = uv * float2(28.0, 58.0);
    normalUV.x += uv.y * (1.4 + windDir.x * 2.6) + windDir.x * _Time.y * 1.7;
    normalUV.y -= _Time.y * (7.5 + windSpeed * 7.0);

    float height = ValueNoise(normalUV);
    float heightX = ValueNoise(normalUV + float2(0.071, 0.0)) - height;
    float heightY = ValueNoise(normalUV + float2(0.0, 0.071)) - height;

    return float2(heightX + windDir.x * 0.05, heightY - 0.08) * (rainIntensity * 0.0065);
}
Verified by source:

One RaycastCommand upward shelter probe, persistent NativeArray<RaycastCommand>[1].
Rain opacity lerps via local exposure.
Lightning directional multiplier resolves through math.lerp(1f, 5f, flashStrength) and _DirectionalLightColor.
Thunder delay uses distance / 343f.
Ocean ripple decal shader uses scrolling Voronoi, no CPU particles.
Weather state uses enum/bitmask, no polymorphic state classes.
